eṉac coṟkum, tokaiccoṟkum poruḷ viḷakkam tarukiṟār.
ca. kurucāmi

6.  ilakkaṇattil etu iṭampeṟa vēṇṭum?
karuttaraṅkat talaippu, kēḷvi uruvattil iruppiṉum palavakaiyāṉa viḷakkaṅkaḷukku iṭam taruvatāka amain tuḷḷatu. karuttaraṅkap poruḷ attakaiyatāka irukkalām; allatu kēḷvi amaippu attakaiya pala poruḷ viḷakkattiṟku iṭam tantirukkalām.
oru moḻiyiṉ colluruvaṅkaḷiṉ aṭippaṭaiyil ilakkaṇam oṉṟu uruvākumpōtu atil vitikaḷum, vitivilakkukaḷum iṭam peṟum. ilakkaṇattai varaiyaṟai ceykiṉṟavarkaḷ avvāṟutāṉ kōṭiṭṭuk kāṭṭuvar. ittakaiya ilakkaṇattil iru mukaṅkaḷuṇṭu; 1. moḻic colluruvam ākiya ataṉ uḷḷīṭu  2. potumaiyāṉa vitikaḷum ciṟappu vitikaḷum aṭaṅkiya ilakkaṇa amaippumuṟai eṉpaṉa ataṉ iru mukaṅkaḷ. maṟṟoru vaḻiyil coṉṉāl vitikaḷum coṟpaṭṭiyalkaḷum aṭaṅkiyavai ilakkaṇam eṉṟu kūṟa iyalum. mutal mukattai uḷḷīṭu eṉṟum iraṇṭāvatu mukattai amaippu nilai eṉṟum nām peyariṭṭuk koḷḷalām.
`etu eṉṟa kēḷvik kuṟiyīṭu, uḷḷīṭṭaik kuṟikkalām; allatu, amaippu nilaiyaik kuṟikkalām. oru moḻiyiṉ colluruvam aḷavaṟṟup parantu kiṭappatu; palatarappaṭṭatu. avaṟṟuḷ evai evai uḷḷīṭāka amaiya vēṇṭum eṉṟu poruḷ ceytukoṇṭāl colluruvam maṭṭum paṟṟiya kēḷviyāka atu māṟum, ataṉaic caṟṟu viḷakka vēṇṭum.
ōr ilakkaṇam ellā moḻi uruvaṅkaḷaiyum uḷḷaṭakki uruvāvatu; allatu pōtumāṉa colluru vaṅkaḷai uḷḷaṭakkuvatu; allatu cila colluruvaṅkaḷai maṭṭum uḷḷaṭakkuvatu eṉṟu mūṉṟāka vēṟupaṭuttiṭa muṭiyum. oru moḻiyiṉ ellāc col uruvaṅkaḷaiyum muṉṉar eḻunta, ippōtu eḻukiṉṟa, varuṅkālattil eḻum uruvaṅkaḷaiyum uḷḷaṭakkuvatu iyalāta amaippākum. ēṉiṉal antac colluruvaṅkaḷ palatarappaṭṭavaiyāka irukkum. anta ilakkaṇattiṉ uḷḷīṭāka vaḻakkukaḷaiyum irumoḻi pēcuvōriṉ pēccaiyum uṭkoṇṭāl vitikaḷ potuvāka amaiyātu. taṉic coṟkaḷaik kuṟippatākap perumpālum amaiyum. malaiyāḷa ilakkaṇa āciriyar ē.ār. irāja irāja varmā taṉatu kēraḷa pāṇiṉīyattil camakiruta uruvaṅkaḷukkum ilakkaṇa viti amaikka muṟpaṭṭatāl potu vitikaḷaṉaittum ciṟappu vitikaḷāka, coṟpaṭṭiyalkaḷāka māṟiṉa. eṉavē oru moḻiyiṉ ellāc colluruvaṅkaḷaiyum uḷḷīṭākak koṇṭāl ilakkaṇam coṟpaṭṭikaiyāka māṟum; potu vitikaḷai vakukka iyalātu.
ataṟku māṟākap pōtiya colluruvaṅkaḷai maṭṭum ōr ilakkaṇam aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭāl, amaippu muṟaiyaic cemmaiyāka amaittiṭa muṭiyum. atu aṭippaṭai yākak koḷḷum collīṭukaḷ tērnteṭukkappaṭṭavaiyāka irukkum. inta muṟaiyaip piṉpaṟṟiṉāl maṟṟoru cikkal eḻum. ilakkaṇattil uḷḷīṭāka amainta celluruvam pōtumāṉatā eṉṟu muṭipu ceyvatu evvāṟu? eṉpatē antac cikkal. ēṟattāḻa mūvāyiram vākkiyaṅkaḷaik koṇṭa uḷḷīṭṭai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭu vakukkappaṭṭa vitikaḷ, ammoḻi uruvaṅkaḷ aṉaittaiyum viḷakkukiṉṟaṉavā eṉṟu cōtikkalām. oru moḻiyiṉ uḷḷīṭu pōtumāṉatā eṉṟu aṟivataṟku, oru kuṟippiṭṭa aḷavilāṉa vākkiyaṅ kaḷaittām aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭuḷḷōm. anta vākkiyaṅkaḷiṉ tokuppu varaiyaṟaiyaṟṟataṉṟu. tiṭṭamāṉatu, iraṇṭāyiram allatu mūvāyiram eṉṟa eṇṇikkai aḷavu peṟṟatu.
āyirattut toḷḷāyirattu aimpatil vāḻnta moḻiyiyalāḷarukkut tiṭṭamāṉa colluruvaṅkaḷ, tappip piḻaikkum oruvaḻi nilaiyāka iruntatu. yārēṉum, oru vitikku māṟupaṭṭa colluruvaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu kuraleḻuppiṉāl, `eṉatu uḷḷīṭṭu vākkiyaṅkaḷil antac colluruvaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭavillai eṉṟu camātāṉam kūṟi  naḻuviṉar. appōtellām tappip piḻaikkum anta vaḻiyai mēṟkoḷvar. `anta uruvam eṉatu vākkiya uḷḷīṭṭil illai allatu `eṉatu kaḷaāyvuk kuṟippil antac colluruvamillai eṉṟu kūṟit tappiṉar. ilakkaṇa vitikaḷukku etir uruvaṅkaḷ iruppatai amaippiyalāḷarkaḷ (ṭrakcuraliṭ) cuṭṭikkāṭṭiṉar. eṉavē ilakkaṇattiṉ iyalpai varaiyaṟai ceyvatu varai, ettakaiya col luruvaṅkaḷ ilakkaṇattil iṭampeṟa vēṇṭum eṉṟu tiṭṭamākak kūṟāmal iruntataipa perum kuṟaiyāka moḻiyiyalāḷarkaḷ karutavillai.
intiya ilakkaṇa āciriyarkaḷaṉaivarum uḷḷīṭṭuc colluruvaṅkaḷiṉ kuṟaikaḷai uṇarntiruntaṉar. ataṉāl tāṉ tamatu ilakkaṇa vitikaḷiṉ iṟutiyil `aṟiñarkaḷiṉ payaṉpāṭṭai nōkkit terintiṭuka allatu `aṟiñarkaḷiṭam kēṭṭut terintu koḷka eṉṟa cūttiramum amaittiruk kiṉṟaṉar. `cēṣam pirayōkātñēyam (iṅkuk kūṟātaṉa vaṟṟaip payaṉpāṭṭāl terintukoḷka); `vallār vāyk kēṭṭuṇarka (iṅkuk kūṟātavaṟṟai aṟiñarkaḷ pāl kēṭṭuṇarka) pōṉṟa cūttiraṅkaḷ allatu viḷakkaṅkaḷ malaiyāḷattilum tamiḻilum marapuvaḻipapaṭṭa ilakkaṇaṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
mūṉṟāvatu kūṟiya kuṟainta aḷavilāṉa uḷḷīṭu, oru kuṟippiṭṭa ilakkaṇap pakuppiṟkup payaṉpaṭuttumpōtu, pōtiyatāka amaiyiṉum motta ilakkaṇattiṟku atu aḷaviṟ kuṟaintatākavē teṉpaṭum. piṟaviṉai uruvaṅ kaḷaṉaittaiyum uḷḷīṭāka amaiyum ilakkaṇattiṟku vākkiya uruvaṅkaḷ aṉaittaiyum atu aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭālum motta ilakkaṇattiṟku anta vākkiya uḷḷīṭukaḷ pōtumāṉavaiyāka amaiyā. pāṭañcollum vakuppukaḷil payaṉpaṭum eṭuttukkāṭṭu uruvaṅkaḷai maṭṭum koṇṭa ilakkaṇam mutirāta kuḻantai nilaiyiluḷḷa ilakkaṇamāka amaiyum. yārum ataṉait takka ilakkaṇamākak karuta māṭṭārkaḷ. eṉavē mūṉṟāvatu viḷakkattai nām nirākarittuviṭalām.
iraṇṭāvatu piriviṟ kūṟiya ilakkaṇattiṉ amaippup paṟṟi ituvarai nāṉ etuvum kūṟavillai. ellā ilakkaṇaṅkaḷilum potu vitikaḷilum ciṟappu vitikaḷum kāṇappaṭum. maṟṟoru vaḻiyil coṉṉāl ovvoru ilakkaṇattilum vitikaḷum coṟkaḷiṉ paṭṭiyalum kāṇappaṭum. uḷḷīṭṭuc coṟkaḷ perukap peruka potu vitikaḷ kuṟaintu ciṟappu vitikaḷ perukum. ciṟappu vitikaḷ allatu coṟpaṭṭiyalaik kuṟaikka, uḷḷīṭṭuc coṟkaḷai aṟiñarkaḷ kuṟaikka muyalvar. `avai taraṅ kuṟaintavai allatu `cīriya aṟiñarkaḷuṭaiya pēccō allatu kēḷviyō alla eṉṟu avaṟṟai nirākarittuviṭuvatu oru vaḻi. piramāṇa ilakkaṇamāka (reḵpareṉ kirāmar) iraṇṭu aṇmaiyil veḷivantuḷḷaṉa. atil oṉṟu, cāmuval mārṭṭiṉ `cappāṉ moḻiyiṉ piramāṇa ilakkaṇam (roḵpareṉ kirāmar āḵp jāpaṉī) eṉṟa nūlil, potu vitikaḷum coṟpaṭṭiyalum tantirukkiṉṟār. vitikku etirāṉa coṟkaḷai varukai muṟaikku ēṟpak koṭuttirukkiṉṟār. āṭṭō yepparcaṉ eḻutiya `āṅkila ilakkaṇattiṉ mukkiya amcam (ecaṉciyal āḵp iṅkilī kirāmar) eṉṟa nūl iraṇṭāvatu nūlākum. anta nūlilum potu vitikaḷum avaṟṟiṟku etirāṉa eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷum tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. eṉavē perukiya uḷḷīṭṭāl potu vitikaḷum etirāṉa coṟpaṭṭiyalumtām koṭuttiṭa muṭiyum eṉpatu teḷivākiṉṟaṉa. puṟanilaiyil (carḵpēḵ leval) avvāṟu tāṉ ceyya iyalum. ellāc coṟkaḷaiyum viti mūlam tōṟṟuvittiṭa muṭiyātu eṉpatu uṟutiyākiṟatu. akanilai amaippai (ṭīpleval) kaṇakkileṭuttu vitiyamaittāl, kūṭutalaḷavil moḻi uruvaṅkaḷukku viti amaittiṭa iyalum eṉṟu ippōtu teḷivāki varukiṟatu. avvāṟu ceyyiṉum vitikku etirāṉa coṟkaḷait tiruptiyāka viḷakka muṭiyumā eṉṟu ammuṟaiyilum teḷivākavillai. viti piḻaitta coṟkaḷ palavāṉāl potu viti māṟṟappaṭum. allatu ciṟappu vitikaḷiṉ eṇṇum mikutiyāka irukkum. vitikku etirāṉa colluruvaṅkaḷ cilavākaiyāl avai puṟakkaṇikkappaṭum; ilakkaṇattiṟku avai oru poruṭṭalla eṉṟu otukkappaṭum.
oru moḻiyiṉ ellā uruvaṅkaḷaiyum - vākkiyaṅkaḷ coṟkaḷ, olikkaṇaṅkaḷ aṉaittaiyum oru ilakkaṇam vivarittu uṟutippaṭutta iyalumā? avvāṟu ceyyum ilakkaṇam eṉṉa vakaiyāṉa ilakkaṇam eṉṟu kūṟa muṭiyumā? vitikaḷaṭaṅkiya ilakkaṇam eṉṟā? allatu coṟpaṭṭiyal ilakkaṇam eṉṟā? attakaiya coṟpaṭṭiyalkaḷaik kaṇippoṟi tokuttut taruvatākak koṇṭālum, antac coṟpaṭṭiyalkaḷaṉaittum oru ilakkaṇattil iṭam peṟumāṟu ceyya iyalumā? eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa kēḷvikaḷ eḻuvatu iyalpu.
ilakkaṇa amaippu iyalpukaḷ paṟṟiya vivātamum viḷakkamum iṉṉum muṟṟuppeṟātatāl muṭinta muṭivāka evvita viḷakkamum antak kēḷvikaḷukku ippōtu aḷikka iyalātu. eṉiṉum oṉṟai maṭṭum uṟutiyākak kūṟa iyalum; palatarappaṭṭa colluruvaṅkaḷai uḷḷīṭāka iṭṭāl ilakkaṇattil potu vitikaḷ kuṟaivākavum coṟpaṭṭiyal allatu ciṟappu vitikaḷ kūṭutalākavum kāṇappaṭum eṉpatu maṭṭum tiṭṭamākak kūṟa iyalum. maṟṟoru vakaiyil kūṟiṉāl ilakkaṇattiṟkum akarātikkumuḷḷa vēṟupāṭu mikak kuṟaivākavē irukkum.
marapuvaḻi ilakkaṇa āciriyarkaḷ, potu vitikaḷiṉ ceyaṟpāṭṭu vallamaiyaip perukka, utti oṉṟaik kaiyāṇṭu vitikaḷai viḷakkukiṉṟaṉar. ataṉ mūlam, perumaḷavil colluruvaṅkaḷai vitiyāl viḷakka muyaṉṟu veṟṟi kāṇkiṉṟaṉar. camakirutattil ataṉai `yukti eṉṟaḻaippar. tolkāppiyar ataṉ pirivukaḷait tokuttuk kūṟiyuḷḷār. avar piṉ vanta yāpparuṅkāla viruttiyiṉ āciriyar ciṟiya māṟupāṭukaḷuṭaṉ avaṟṟait tokuttuḷḷār. naṉṉūlārum avaṟṟai varicaippaṭuttiyuḷḷār. attakaiya uttikaḷaik kauṭilīyariṉ artta cāttiramum, āyurvēta nūlkaḷum tokuttuḷḷaṉa. caṭṭa nipuṇarkaḷ caṭṭattai viḷakkum pōtu oru viḷakka muṟaiyaip piṉpaṟṟuvataiyum nām iṅkē niṉaivu kūrvatu naṉṟu. aracāṅka amaippu muṟai pala ākaiyāl, artta cāttiramum anta uttikaḷaip piṉpaṟṟukiṉṟatu. nōykaḷum, nōy nīkkum maruntum palatarappaṭṭavai ākaiyāl āyurvētamum avaṟṟaip piṉpaṟṟukiṉṟatu. caṭṭa vitikaḷai uḷḷaṭakkum vaḻakkukaḷ kaṇakkaṟṟavai. ākaiyāl vaḻakku maṉṟattil nītipatikaḷum vaḻakkaṟiñarkaḷum oruvakaiyāṉa uttimuṟaiyaik kaiyāṇṭu caṭṭavitikaḷai viḷakki virivākkukiṉṟaṉar. avaṟṟaip pōṉṟē uḷḷīṭṭuc coṟkaḷ kaṇakkaṟṟavaiyāka iruppatāl vitikaḷai utti mūlam virivākki ilakkaṇattil ceyalpaṭuttukiṉṟaṉar.
tolkāppiyam mutaliya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ kūṟum uttikaḷaip piṉ iṇaippil avaṟṟiṉ poruḷuṭaṉ varicaippaṭuttiyuḷḷēṉ. uttikaḷaik kaiyāṇṭu viḷakkam kūṟum vaḻikaḷ iruvakaippaṭum. ilakkaṇam kūṟum vitikaḷil uḷḷa kuṟaipāṭukaḷai nīkkap payaṉpaṭuttal 2. putiya colluruvaṅkaḷ avvappōtu tōṉṟumpōtu avaṟṟaiyum vitikkuḷ aṭakkal ākiya iru muṉaikaḷil uttikaḷaip payaṉpaṭuttukiṉṟaṉar. ilakkaṇa vitikaḷ anta uttikaḷāl nirantara nilaiyaip peṟukiṉṟaṉa. ataṉ mūlam ovvoru ilakkaṇamum kālattāl maṅkāta nilaiyaip peṟukiṟatu. āṉāl anta uttikaḷaip payaṉpaṭutti vitikaḷai virivākkuvatu allatu curukkuvatu pōṉṟa muyaṟcikaḷai uraiyāciriyarkaḷtām ceytiṭa vēṇṭum. eṉavē anta viḷakkattai uraiyāciriyarkaḷ cila muṟaikaḷaip piṉpaṟṟic ceykiṉṟaṉar. cintaṉai vaḷamuṭaiya uraiyāciriyarkaḷāl ōr ilakkaṇam, ataṉ mūlam, nirantaram peṟukiṉṟatu. virinta ceyaṟpāṭṭut tiṟamaiyaiyum peṟukiṉṟatu.
karuttaraṅkat tuvakkavuraiyai āṟṟupavarkaḷ tamatu uraic curukkattāl piṟar pārāṭṭaip peṟum uttiyait terintu koḷvatu nallatu. pala amarvukaḷil ilakkaṇa amcaṅkaḷai virivāka ārāya irukkum uṅkaḷiṭam uttikaḷaip paṟṟiya viḷakkattaiyum, ilakkaṇattil avai peṟum iṭattaiyum virivākak kūṟuvatu uṅkaḷ nērattai vīṇākkum eṉṟu añcukiṟēṉ. yārēṉum uttikaḷaip paṟṟi iṅkē kēḷvi yeḻuppiṉāl piṉṉar nāṉ viḷakka muyalkiṟēṉ. marapu ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ aṉaittum vitikaḷai ekkālattiṟkum ēṟṟavaiyākap pōṟṟavē virumpukiṉṟaṉa. viṭupaṭṭa colluruvaṅkaḷai vitikkuḷ aṭakkuvataṟkum varuṅkālattil  paḻakkattil varum colluruvaṅkaḷait tām kūṟiya vitiyāl viḷakkavum muyalkiṉṟaṉa. ataṉāl uraikārarkaḷum tamatu aṟivut tiṟamaiyālum viḷakka āṟṟalālum mukkiya iṭamum matippum peṟukiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl avarkaḷ peṟum pārāṭṭu, cūttirakārarai aṭuttuttāṉ. eṉavē marapu ilakkaṇaṅkaḷukku iru taḷaṅkaḷ uṇṭu. 1. cūttirakārariṉ ilakkaṇam 2. viruttiyuraikārariṉ ilakkaṇam eṉpavaiyē attaḷaṅkaḷ.
mēlē kūṟiya viḷakkaṅkaḷāl ellā marapu ilakkaṇaṅ kaḷaiyum kālaṅkaṭantavai eṉṟu kūṟiviṭa muṭiyātu. āṉāl tiṟamaiyāṉa viruttiyuraikāraraiyuṭaiya ilakkaṇam kālaṅ kaṭantu niṟkum; nirantaramāka māṟum taṉmai uṭaiyatu eṉak kūṟa muṭiyum.
piṟcērkkai
1. nutaliyatu aṟital: `cūttirattiṟkēṟpak karuti uṇarappaṭum poruḷ iṉṉateṉṟu koḷḷa vaittal.
2. atikāra muṟaimai: `muṉ cūttirap poruḷ piṉ cūttiraṅkaḷ perumpālāṉavaṟṟiṟkum peṟa vaittal.
3. tokuttuk kūṟal: `tokuttu yātta nūlilum teḷiviṟkākat tokuttuk kūṟal.
4. vakuttu mey niṟuttal: `tokuttavaṟṟai vakai ceytu kūṟal; eṭuttukkāṭṭu, iṉam avai, valliṉam, melliṉam, iṭaiyiṉam eṉṟal.
5. avvayiṉ moḻiyātataṉai moḻinta poruḷōṭu oṉṟa muṭṭiṉṟi muṭittal; `eṭuttōtiya poruṇmaikku ēṟpa apporuḷil collāta oṉṟaiyum koḷḷa vaittal.
6. varātataṉai vantatu muṭittal: ilakkaṇam varāta cūttirattāṉ aṅṅaṉam vanta poruṇmaikku vēṇṭum muṭivu koḷḷac ceytal.
7. vantatu koṇṭu vārātatuṇarttal `piṉṉoru vaḻi vantatu koṇṭu muṉ varātatōr poruḷaṟiya vaittal.
8. muntu meḻintataṉ talaitaṭumāṟṟam; `muṉkūṟiya muṟaiyallāmal piṉṉoru kāl talai taṭumāṟṟamākak kūṟutal.
9. oppuk kūṟal: `oṉṟu kūṟumpōtu iruporuḷ kuṟittateṉṟu iraṭṭuṟac ceytal..
10. orutalai moḻital: `oru atikārattil colla vēṇṭuvataṉai vēṟu atikārattil kāṇappaṭum ilakkaṇam poruntum eṉṟu aṅkē koḷḷa vaittal.
11. taṉ kōṭ kūṟal: `collātaṉa palavāyiṉum annūlukku vēṇṭuvaṉavaṟṟai maṭṭum kūṟutal.
12. muṟai piṟaḻāmai: `cila poruḷaik kāraṇamiṉṟi varicaippaṭuttiya piṉṉar avvaricaiyiṉaiyē piṉpaṟṟi ilakkaṇam kūṟal.
13. piṟaṉ uṭampaṭṭatu tāṉuṭampaṭutal; `vaḻakkil evvāṟu amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷatō ataip pōṉṟu tāṉum amaittal.
14. ciṟantatu kāttal: `muṉ kūṟiya cūttirap poruḷaip piṉṉoru cūttirattāl viḷakkutal.
15. etaratu pōṟṟal: `varukiṉṟa cūttirattiṉ poruḷukkēṟpa vēṟoru poruḷai muṉṉē kūṟutal.
16. moḻivāmeṉṟal; `oru payaṉ karuti muṉṉē kūṟuvōm eṉṟu collal.
17. kūṟiṟṟeṉṟal; muṉkūṟiya ilakkaṇattai maṟṟoru poruḷukkuk kūṟumpōtu mīṇṭum anta cūttirattaik kūṟātu muṉkūṟiya cūttirattāṉ koḷḷutal.
18. taṉ kuṟiyiṭutal `ulakak kuṟiyiṉṟit taṉṉūluḷ vēṟu kuṟiyiṭṭu āḷal.
19. orutalaiyiṉmai; `ellā iṭattum oruppōla vārātu, varum poḻutu aṅkē varuvatu eṉṟal.
20. muṭintatu kāṭṭal; `ellām viḷakkamākak kūṟātu, tollāciriyar kūṟiṉāreṉṟu collutal.
21. āṇai kūṟal: `ivvāciriyariṉ karuttu itutāṉ eṉṟu uṟutiyākak koḷḷa vaittal.
22. palporuṭ kēṭpiṉ nallatu kōṭal: `pala poruḷil nallataik koḷḷal.
23. tokutta moḻiyāṉ vakuttaṉar kōṭal: `oru vāypāṭu eṭuttu ōtap pala vāypāṭu ataṟku vantu pūṇum eṉṟu vakuttukkoḷḷa vaittal..
24. maṟutalai citaittut taṉ tuṇivu uraittal: `oruvaṉ oru poruḷai vēṟupaṭak kūṟiṉ avvēṟupāṭṭiṉai māṟṟit tāṉ tuṇintavāṟu avaṉukkuk kūṟal.
25. piṟaṉkōḷ kūṟal: `taṉṉūl aṭippaṭaiyil piṟa nūlukku ilakkaṇam koḷṟumāṟu kūṟal.
26. aṟiyātu uṭampaṭal: `tāṉ kūṟiya ilakkaṇattiṟku vēṟupaṭa varuvaṉa tāṉ aṟiyavillai eṉṟu kūṟi ataṉaip puṟanaṭaiyāk kūṟal.
27. poruḷiṭai iṭutal: `collukiṉṟa poruḷiṉ iṭaiyē vēṟoru poruḷaic collutalum collukiṉṟa poruḷukku ēṟṟa poruḷai āṇṭuc collātu piṟitoru vaḻic collutalum.
28. etirporuḷuṇarttal: muṉṉūṟkaḷāl ilakkaṇat tiṟku tiripu paṭum eṉṟu uṇarntu etirkālattiṟ kēṟṟatōr ilakkaṇam ceytal.
29. colliṉ eccam colliyāṅku uṇarttal; `colliṉ āṟṟalāl peṟappaṭum poruḷaiyum eṭuttu ōtiya muṟaiyil koḷḷa vaittal.
30. tantu puṇarnturaittal: `uḷporuḷallātataṉai uḷa pōlat tantu kūṭa uṇarttal.
31. ñāpakaṅ kūṟal: `curukkamākac collātu aritum peritumāka nalintu ceytu maṟṟataṉāṉē vēṟupala poruḷ uṇarttal.
32. uyttuk koṇṭuṇarttal: `oru poruḷaic collumpōtu ataṉ kaṇṇē maṟṟoru poruḷaik koṇarntu koṇṭaṟiyumeṉṟu tōṉṟac ceytal.
uttikaḷ muppattiraṇṭukkum mēṟpaṭṭavai eṉṟu
 nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uṇarntuḷḷataiyum iṅkē kuṟippiṭa vēṇṭum.
yāpparuṅkāla viruttiyil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa muppattiraṇṭu uttikaḷum pāṭalāṉār kūṟiyavai eṉṟu kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu eṉṟum, avaraip piṉpaṟṟi navanītap pāṭṭiyal uraiyilum ciṅkāra vaṭivēlu mutaliyār ceyta apitāṉa cintāmaṇiyilum kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa eṉṟum kēraḷap palkalaikkaḻakat tamiḻt tuṟai āyvu māṇavar tiru. caṇmukam kūṟiṉār. pāṭalaṉār eṉṟa peyar pāṭaliyār eṉṟiruntatāl, artta cāttiram ceyta kauṭilīyar pāṭaliputtiraṉār ākaiyāl avaraik kuṟikkalām eṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu. artta cāttira uttikaḷuṭaṉ mēlē kuṟippiṭṭavaṟṟai iṉimēltāṉ oppunōkka vēṇṭum.
- va.ay. cupramaṇiyam kaṭṭuraikaḷ,
pak. 49- 56
tokuppu: aruṇ cuppiramaṇiyaṉ
